# Location Resolver Full-Catalog Backfill — 2026-10-01

Source: re-running `resolveEstateWebLocationFromSources` (current, fixed resolver, including root cause #14 and the 14 newly-set `agencyCity` scopes) against every property's live Google geocode, compared to what's currently stored.

**Total properties that would change: 718 / 5,643**
- **HIGH confidence (written automatically below): 442**
- **LOW confidence (needs manual review before writing): 276**

## How confidence was decided

- **HIGH**: either (a) the property has real coordinates and a live Google reverse-geocode of those coordinates independently confirms the new answer's prefecture, or (b) no coordinates, but the agency has an `agencyCity` scope set and the new answer falls inside that scope.
- **LOW**: anything else — no coordinates and no agency scope (pure text-matching, the same "polluted forward-geocode" risk documented as root cause #11), coordinates that *disagree* with the resolver's own answer (a real red flag — needs investigation, not just a reprocessing gap), the two agencies deliberately left unscoped (`cretahouses`, `asiminarealestate` — no single dominant region), or a path too shallow to read a prefecture from.
